# Main Splat
vampire = {
	flag = splat
	prowess_no_portrait = 5
	max_hostile_schemes_add = 1
	
	flag = immortality
	immortal = yes
	can_have_children = no
	flag = can_murder_own_children
	
	opposites = {
		use_ingame_chargen
		kueijin
		ghoul
		revenant
		supehunter
		mortal
		shapeshifter
		mage #lilith
		fae
		changeling
		demon
		wraith
		mummy
	}
	
	triggered_opinion = {
		parameter = vampirism_illegal
		opinion_modifier = vampirism_crime
		ignore_opinion_value_if_same_trait = yes
	}
	
	triggered_opinion = {
		parameter = vampirism_shunned
		opinion_modifier = vampirism_intolerant
		ignore_opinion_value_if_same_trait = yes
	}	
	
	triggered_opinion = {
		parameter = hunter_hunting_vampire
		opinion_modifier = hunter_hunting
		ignore_opinion_value_if_same_trait = yes
	}

	name = {
		first_valid = {		
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { NOT = { exists = this } }
				desc = trait_vampire
			}	
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_global_variable = prodigal }
				desc = trait_prodigal
			}
			desc = trait_vampire
		}
	}
	
	desc = {
		first_valid = {	
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { NOT = { exists = this } }
				desc = trait_vampire_desc
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_global_variable = prodigal }
				desc = trait_prodigal_vampire_desc
			}	
			desc = trait_vampire_desc
		}
	}
	
	ai_war_cooldown = 0.5
	ai_war_chance = -0.25
}

				desc = trait_hakken_desc
			}
			desc = trait_werewolf_desc
		}
	}
